# Translated-box wall falsification probe

This report executes the translated-window, all-level gauge, and operator-norm protocol of Q6573 sections 17--18 and 21 on the exact non-wrapping triangle `0 <= r < r+h <= p-2`.

## Conventions and validation

For `I=(H0,H0+H]`, `d_I(r)` counts zero determinants among admissible h and `q_I(r)` counts the corresponding admissible pairs. For a column h, `J_h` is the subset of J remaining in the triangle. The 8 translated J-windows have nearest-integer length p/4 and evenly spaced starts; h-window starts span the full interval from 1 through the rounded `4 p^(2/3)` endpoint.

- Orbit code cross-check: all 16110 pairs at p=181 satisfy `Delta=0 iff N_h(r)=0` (197 collisions).
- Banked statistic: at p=1009, `E^pi=3030` and `E^pi/p=3.002973` (exact expected energy 3030).
- The direct polynomial recurrence through h=12 agrees with `lc(N_1)=1`, `lc(N_2)=34`, and `lc(N_(h+1))=34 lc(N_h)-lc(N_(h-1))`.
- Gauge g2 omits an h-column when its leading coefficient is 0 modulo p; gauge g3 uses only pairs where both b-coordinates are nonzero. Each gauge is normalized by its own domain size, and the retained coverage is reported.
- SVD matrices set entries outside the triangular domain to zero and discard all-zero outer rows/columns. Eligible effective shapes are at most 2000 by 2000. Each random replica is iid Bernoulli with the wall reference mean `1/p` on the same mask and uses the same `1/p` centering.

## Failure-mode verdicts

The finite-scan detection thresholds used here are `V0/|I| > 3` for a localized row anomaly, all-level defect/|Omega| > 3 for level concentration, and the protocol's explicit data/random SVD ratio > 3 for a weighted mode. Values below these thresholds are evidence only at the scanned primes and translations.

| failure mode | verdict | numerical evidence |
|:---|:---|:---|
| localized heavy row | not detected at these scales | max V0/|I|=2.459 in p1009-p1_3-H10-i06-Jfull; extreme row r=28 has d=1 of q=10 |
| thin resonance class | not detected at these scales | largest data/random singular ratio=2.808 in p3001-p1_3-H14-i05-Jt04 |
| nonzero-level concentration / gauge dependence | not detected at these scales | g1_raw max=1.588 (p1009-p2_3-H101-i01-Jt03); g2_lc max=1.25 (p1009-p1_2-H32-i04-Jfull); g3_chart max=1.442 (p1009-p2_3-H101-i01-Jt03) |
| weighted singular vector | not detected (no ratio > 3) | max ratio=2.808; data=1, benchmark=0.3561 |
